1. using System.Diagnostics;
2. using Microsoft.Win32;
3. 
4. namespace TaskbarAlignmentTool;
5. 
6. /// <summary>
7. /// Application context that manages the system-tray icon, context menu,
8. /// and wires the display monitor to the taskbar aligner using resolution profiles.
9. /// </summary>
10. internal sealed class TrayApplicationContext : ApplicationContext
11. {
12.     private const string StartupRegistryPath = @"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run";
13.     private const string StartupValueName = "TaskbarAlignmentTool";
14. 
15.     private AppConfig _config;
16.     private readonly DisplayMonitor _monitor;
17.     private readonly NotifyIcon _notifyIcon;
18.     private readonly ToolStripMenuItem _effectiveResItem;
19.     private readonly ToolStripMenuItem _nativeResItem;
20.     private readonly ToolStripMenuItem _profileItem;
21.     private readonly ToolStripMenuItem _startupItem;
22. 
23.     private static readonly Lazy<bool> _isMsixPackaged = new(DetectMsixPackaged);
24. 
25.     private int _profileSwitchCount;
26. 
27.     public TrayApplicationContext(AppConfig config)
28.     {
29.         _config = config;
30.         _monitor = new DisplayMonitor(config.RefreshIntervalMs, config.ResolutionMode);
31. 
32.         _effectiveResItem = new ToolStripMenuItem("Effective: —") { Enabled = false };
33.         _nativeResItem = new ToolStripMenuItem("Native: —") { Enabled = false };
34.         _profileItem = new ToolStripMenuItem("Profile: \u2014") { Enabled = false };
35.         _startupItem = new ToolStripMenuItem("Run at Startup", null, OnToggleStartup)
36.         {
37.             Checked = IsStartupEnabled()
38.         };
39. 
40.         var menu = new ContextMenuStrip();
41.         menu.Items.Add(_effectiveResItem);
42.         menu.Items.Add(_nativeResItem);
43.         menu.Items.Add(new ToolStripSeparator());
44.         menu.Items.Add(_profileItem);
45.         menu.Items.Add(new ToolStripSeparator());
46.         menu.Items.Add("Open Config", null, OnOpenConfig);
47.         menu.Items.Add("Reload Config", null, OnReloadConfig);
48.         menu.Items.Add(new ToolStripSeparator());
49.         menu.Items.Add(_startupItem);
50.         menu.Items.Add("Refresh Now", null, OnRefresh);
51.         menu.Items.Add(new ToolStripSeparator());
52.         menu.Items.Add(CreateDiagnosticsMenu());
53.         menu.Items.Add(new ToolStripSeparator());
54.         menu.Items.Add("Exit", null, OnExit);
55. 
56.         _notifyIcon = new NotifyIcon
57.         {
58.             Icon = RenderTrayIcon(0),
59.             ContextMenuStrip = menu,
60.             Text = "Taskbar Alignment Tool",
61.             Visible = true
62.         };
63. 
64.         _monitor.DisplayInfoChanged += OnDisplayInfoChanged;
65. 
66.         // Apply profile immediately on startup
67.         ApplyForWidth(_monitor.EffectiveWidth);
68.     }
69. 
70.     private void OnDisplayInfoChanged(object? sender, DisplayInfo info)
71.     {
72.         ApplyForWidth(info.EffectiveWidth);
73.     }
74. 
75.     private void ApplyForWidth(int effectiveWidth)
76.     {
77.         var profile = _config.ResolveProfile(effectiveWidth);
78.         if (profile != null && TaskbarAligner.ApplyProfile(profile))
79.         {
80.             _profileSwitchCount++;
81.             if (_config.ShowNotifications)
82.             {
83.                 _notifyIcon.ShowBalloonTip(
84.                     _config.NotificationDurationMs,
85.                     "Taskbar Alignment Tool",
86.                     $"Switched to \"{profile.Name}\" ({effectiveWidth}px)",
87.                     ToolTipIcon.Info);
88.             }
89.         }
90.         var displayInfo = _monitor.CurrentDisplayInfo;
91.         UpdateStatus(displayInfo, profile);
92.     }
93. 
94.     private void UpdateStatus(DisplayInfo info, ProfileConfig? profile)
95.     {
96.         var profileName = profile?.Name ?? "None";
97.         _effectiveResItem.Text = $"Effective: {info.EffectiveWidth}\u00d7{info.EffectiveHeight}";
98.         _nativeResItem.Text = $"Native: {info.NativeWidth}\u00d7{info.NativeHeight} @ {info.ScalingPercent}%";
99.         _profileItem.Text = $"Profile: {profileName}";
100. 
101.         // Format: "ProfileName | 1920×1080 eff (3840×2160 @ 200%)"
102.         var resText = $"{info.EffectiveWidth}\u00d7{info.EffectiveHeight} eff ({info.NativeWidth}\u00d7{info.NativeHeight} @ {info.ScalingPercent}%)";
103.         var tooltip = $"{profileName} | {resText}";
104.         // Truncate profile name if tooltip exceeds 127 chars (NotifyIcon.Text limit is 127 + null)
105.         if (tooltip.Length > 127)
106.         {
107.             var maxName = 127 - " | ".Length - resText.Length - "\u2026".Length;
108.             if (maxName > 0)
109.                 tooltip = $"{profileName[..maxName]}\u2026 | {resText}";
110.             else
111.                 tooltip = resText[..Math.Min(resText.Length, 127)];
112.         }
113.         _notifyIcon.Text = tooltip;
114. 
115.         // Update tray icon with current width
116.         var oldIcon = _notifyIcon.Icon;
117.         _notifyIcon.Icon = RenderTrayIcon(info.EffectiveWidth);
118.         oldIcon?.Dispose();
119.     }
120. 
121.     private void OnRefresh(object? sender, EventArgs e)
122.     {
123.         _monitor.Refresh(force: true);
124.     }
125. 
126.     private void OnOpenConfig(object? sender, EventArgs e)
127.     {
128.         var path = AppConfig.GetConfigPath();
129.         if (!File.Exists(path))
130.             _config.Save();
131.         Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(path) { UseShellExecute = true });
132.     }
133. 
134.     private void OnReloadConfig(object? sender, EventArgs e)
135.     {
136.         _config = AppConfig.Load();
137.         _monitor.UpdateInterval(_config.RefreshIntervalMs, _config.ResolutionMode);
138.         _monitor.Refresh(force: true);
139.     }
140. 
141.     private void OnToggleStartup(object? sender, EventArgs e)
142.     {
143.         if (IsStartupEnabled())
144.             DisableStartup();
145.         else
146.             EnableStartup();
147. 
148.         _startupItem.Checked = IsStartupEnabled();
149.     }
150. 
151.     private static bool IsMsixPackaged() => _isMsixPackaged.Value;
152. 
153.     private static bool DetectMsixPackaged()
154.     {
155.         try
156.         {
157.             // Windows.ApplicationModel.Package.Current throws if not packaged
158.             _ = Windows.ApplicationModel.Package.Current.Id;
159.             return true;
160.         }
161.         catch
162.         {
163.             return false;
164.         }
165.     }
166. 
167.     private static bool IsStartupEnabled()
168.     {
169.         if (IsMsixPackaged())
170.         {
171.             try
172.             {
173.                 var task = Windows.ApplicationModel.StartupTask
174.                     .GetAsync("TaskbarAlignmentToolStartup").GetAwaiter().GetResult();
175.                 return task.State == Windows.ApplicationModel.StartupTaskState.Enabled;
176.             }
177.             catch { return false; }
178.         }
179. 
180.         using var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(StartupRegistryPath, false);
181.         return key?.GetValue(StartupValueName) != null;
182.     }
183. 
184.     private static void EnableStartup()
185.     {
186.         if (IsMsixPackaged())
187.         {
188.             try
189.             {
190.                 var task = Windows.ApplicationModel.StartupTask
191.                     .GetAsync("TaskbarAlignmentToolStartup").GetAwaiter().GetResult();
192.                 task.RequestEnableAsync().GetAwaiter().GetResult();
193.             }
194.             catch { /* Startup task not available */ }
195.             return;
196.         }
197. 
198.         using var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(StartupRegistryPath, true);
199.         var exePath = Application.ExecutablePath;
200.         key?.SetValue(StartupValueName, $"\"{exePath}\"");
201.     }
202. 
203.     private static void DisableStartup()
204.     {
205.         if (IsMsixPackaged())
206.         {
207.             try
208.             {
209.                 var task = Windows.ApplicationModel.StartupTask
210.                     .GetAsync("TaskbarAlignmentToolStartup").GetAwaiter().GetResult();
211.                 task.Disable();
212.             }
213.             catch { /* Startup task not available */ }
214.             return;
215.         }
216. 
217.         using var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(StartupRegistryPath, true);
218.         key?.DeleteValue(StartupValueName, false);
219.     }
220. 
221.     private ToolStripMenuItem CreateDiagnosticsMenu()
222.     {
223.         var diag = new ToolStripMenuItem("Diagnostics");
224.         diag.DropDownOpening += (_, _) =>
225.         {
226.             diag.DropDownItems.Clear();
227.             var proc = System.Diagnostics.Process.GetCurrentProcess();
228.             var memMb = proc.WorkingSet64 / (1024.0 * 1024.0);
229.             var cpuTime = proc.TotalProcessorTime;
230.             diag.DropDownItems.Add(new ToolStripMenuItem($"Memory: {memMb:F1} MB") { Enabled = false });
231.             diag.DropDownItems.Add(new ToolStripMenuItem($"CPU time: {cpuTime.TotalSeconds:F2}s") { Enabled = false });
232.             diag.DropDownItems.Add(new ToolStripMenuItem($"Profile switches: {_profileSwitchCount}") { Enabled = false });
233.         };
234.         return diag;
235.     }
236. 
237.     private void OnExit(object? sender, EventArgs e)
238.     {
239.         _notifyIcon.Visible = false;
240.         _monitor.Dispose();
241.         _notifyIcon.Dispose();
242.         Application.Exit();
243.     }
244. 
245.     /// <summary>
246.     /// Renders a DPI-aware tray icon showing the current display width.
247.     /// Auto-detects Windows light/dark theme for contrast.
248.     /// </summary>
249.     private static Icon RenderTrayIcon(int width)
250.     {
251.         int dpi = GetSystemDpi();
252.         int size = (int)(16 * dpi / 96.0);
253.         float scale = size / 16f;
254.         bool isDarkTheme = IsSystemDarkTheme();
255. 
256.         using var bmp = new Bitmap(size, size);
257.         using var g = Graphics.FromImage(bmp);
258.         g.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.HighQuality;
259.         g.TextRenderingHint = System.Drawing.Text.TextRenderingHint.ClearTypeGridFit;
260.         g.PixelOffsetMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.PixelOffsetMode.HighQuality;
261.         g.Clear(Color.Transparent);
262. 
263.         // Background color adapts to theme
264.         var bgColor = isDarkTheme
265.             ? Color.FromArgb(255, 0, 150, 180)   // Teal on dark taskbar
266.             : Color.FromArgb(255, 0, 120, 150);  // Darker teal on light taskbar
267.         var textBrush = isDarkTheme ? Brushes.White : Brushes.White;
268. 
269.         using var bgBrush = new SolidBrush(bgColor);
270.         g.FillRectangle(bgBrush, 0, 0, size, size);
271. 
272.         // Display width as the full icon content
273.         var label = width.ToString();
274.         float fontSize = label.Length <= 3 ? 7f : label.Length == 4 ? 5.5f : 4.5f;
275.         using var font = new Font("Segoe UI", fontSize * scale, FontStyle.Bold, GraphicsUnit.Pixel);
276.         var sf = new StringFormat
277.         {
278.             Alignment = StringAlignment.Center,
279.             LineAlignment = StringAlignment.Center
280.         };
281.         var textRect = new RectangleF(0, 0, size, size);
282.         g.DrawString(label, font, textBrush, textRect, sf);
283. 
284.         return Icon.FromHandle(bmp.GetHicon());
285.     }
286. 
287.     /// <summary>
288.     /// Checks if Windows is using dark mode for apps.
289.     /// Registry: HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Themes\Personalize\SystemUsesLightTheme
290.     /// 0 = dark, 1 = light
291.     /// </summary>
292.     private static bool IsSystemDarkTheme()
293.     {
294.         try
295.         {
296.             using var key = Microsoft.Win32.Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(
297.                 @"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Themes\Personalize", false);
298.             var value = key?.GetValue("SystemUsesLightTheme");
299.             if (value is int intVal)
300.                 return intVal == 0;
301.         }
302.         catch { }
303.         return true; // Default to dark
304.     }
305. 
306.     private static int GetSystemDpi()
307.     {
308.         try
309.         {
310.             var hMonitor = NativeMethods.MonitorFromPoint(0, NativeMethods.MONITOR_DEFAULTTOPRIMARY);
311.             if (hMonitor != nint.Zero &&
312.                 NativeMethods.GetDpiForMonitor(hMonitor, NativeMethods.MDT_EFFECTIVE_DPI, out uint dpiX, out _) == 0 &&
313.                 dpiX > 0)
314.             {
315.                 return (int)dpiX;
316.             }
317.         }
318.         catch { }
319.         return 96;
320.     }
321. }
322. 
```

```
75.     private void ApplyForWidth(int effectiveWidth)
76.     {
77.         var profile = _config.ResolveProfile(effectiveWidth);
78.         if (profile != null && TaskbarAligner.ApplyProfile(profile))
79.         {
80.             _profileSwitchCount++;
81.             if (_config.ShowNotifications)
82.             {
83.                 _notifyIcon.ShowBalloonTip(
84.                     _config.NotificationDurationMs,
85.                     "Taskbar Alignment Tool",
86.                     $"Switched to \"{profile.Name}\" ({_monitor.CurrentDisplayInfo.EffectiveWidth}\u00d7{_monitor.CurrentDisplayInfo.EffectiveHeight})",
87.                     ToolTipIcon.Info);
88.             }
89.         }
90.         var displayInfo = _monitor.CurrentDisplayInfo;
91.         UpdateStatus(displayInfo, profile);
92.     }
93. 
94.     private void UpdateStatus(DisplayInfo info, ProfileConfig? profile)
95.     {
96.         var profileName = profile?.Name ?? "None";
97.         bool unavailable = info.EffectiveWidth == 0 && info.EffectiveHeight == 0;
98. 
99.         _effectiveResItem.Text = unavailable
100.             ? "Resolution: unavailable"
101.             : $"Effective: {info.EffectiveWidth}\u00d7{info.EffectiveHeight}";
102.         _nativeResItem.Text = unavailable
103.             ? string.Empty
104.             : $"Native: {info.NativeWidth}\u00d7{info.NativeHeight} @ {info.ScalingPercent}%";
105.         _nativeResItem.Visible = !unavailable;
106.         _profileItem.Text = $"Profile: {profileName}";
107. 
108.         if (unavailable)
109.         {
110.             _notifyIcon.Text = "Resolution: unavailable";
111.             return;
112.         }
113. 
114.         // Format: "ProfileName | 1920×1080 eff (3840×2160 @ 200%)"
115.         var resText = $"{info.EffectiveWidth}\u00d7{info.EffectiveHeight} eff ({info.NativeWidth}\u00d7{info.NativeHeight} @ {info.ScalingPercent}%)";
116.         var tooltip = $"{profileName} | {resText}";
117.         // Truncate profile name if tooltip exceeds 127 chars (NotifyIcon.Text limit is 127 + null)
118.         if (tooltip.Length > 127)
119.         {
120.             var maxName = 127 - " | ".Length - resText.Length - "\u2026".Length;
121.             if (maxName > 0)
122.                 tooltip = $"{profileName[..maxName]}\u2026 | {resText}";
123.             else
124.                 tooltip = resText[..Math.Min(resText.Length, 127)];
125.         }
126.         _notifyIcon.Text = tooltip;
127. 
128.         // Update tray icon with current width
129.         var oldIcon = _notifyIcon.Icon;
130.         _notifyIcon.Icon = RenderTrayIcon(info.EffectiveWidth);
```

</details>

- `DisplayMonitor.cs` — Added `DisplayInfo` record, `GetDisplayInfo()` method capturing dpiY for height calc, `CurrentDisplayInfo` property, `DisplayInfoChanged` event
- `TrayApplicationContext.cs` — Tooltip shows `"Profile | W×H eff (W×H @ Scale%)"` with 127-char truncation; context menu has `Effective:` and `Native:` disabled items at top; balloon notification shows W×H; "Resolution: unavailable" fallback for null screen
- `README.md` — Updated Features and Run sections for new tooltip/menu behavior
- `AppConfig.cs`, `TaskbarAligner.cs`, `schema/config.schema.json` — Supporting changes from prior session

**15/20 tasks complete** (5 remaining are manual hardware verification per quickstart.md). Build passes with 0 errors.

94.     private void UpdateStatus(DisplayInfo info, ProfileConfig? profile)
95.     {
96.         var profileName = profile?.Name ?? "None";
97.         bool unavailable = info.EffectiveWidth == 0 && info.EffectiveHeight == 0;
98. 
99.         _effectiveResItem.Text = unavailable
100.             ? "Resolution: unavailable"
101.             : $"Effective: {info.EffectiveWidth}\u00d7{info.EffectiveHeight}";
102.         _nativeResItem.Text = unavailable
103.             ? string.Empty
104.             : $"Native: {info.NativeWidth}\u00d7{info.NativeHeight} @ {info.ScalingPercent}%";
105.         _nativeResItem.Visible = !unavailable;
106.         _profileItem.Text = $"Profile: {profileName}";
107. 
108.         if (unavailable)
109.         {
110.             _notifyIcon.Text = "Resolution: unavailable";
111.             return;
112.         }
113. 
114.         // Format: "ProfileName | 1920×1080 eff (3840×2160 @ 200%)"
115.         var resText = $"{info.EffectiveWidth}\u00d7{info.EffectiveHeight} eff ({info.NativeWidth}\u00d7{info.NativeHeight} @ {info.ScalingPercent}%)";
116.         var tooltip = $"{profileName} | {resText}";
117.         // Truncate profile name if tooltip exceeds 127 chars (NotifyIcon.Text limit is 127 + null)
118.         if (tooltip.Length > 127)
119.         {
120.             var maxName = 127 - " | ".Length - resText.Length - "\u2026".Length;
121.             if (maxName > 0)
122.                 tooltip = $"{profileName[..maxName]}\u2026 | {resText}";
123.             else
124.                 tooltip = resText[..Math.Min(resText.Length, 127)];
125.         }
126.         _notifyIcon.Text = tooltip;
127.
```
